=== LEGION XVI ROOT / REFERENCE ===
selectionEntry legion-xvi | XVI Legion — Sons of Horus | type=upgrade hidden=false costs=[('Points', '0')] cons=[('legion-xvi-max', 'max', 'selections', '1', 'parent')] cats=[]
  RULE legion-xvi-named | Legiones Astartes (Sons of Horus) | hidden=false | This Detachment is Sons of Horus. Models with the Legiones Astartes special rule gain Legiones Astartes (Sons of Horus). Legion-specific rules, units, wargear and characters are unlocked by this selection where applicable.
  RULE r25-legion-xvi-reference | Sons Of Horus — Legion Rules & Armoury | hidden=false | LEGION SPECIAL RULES CLOSE RANGE BRUTALITY The Sons of Horus favour overwhelming close-range assaults in which gunfire and blade work become almost indistinguishable. At the beginning of the Assault phase, a Sons of Horus unit which is otherwise able to charge may fire at the enemy unit it intends to charge. Models armed with Rapid Fire, Pistol or Assault weapons may fire. Template weapons may not be used. These attacks hit only on a 5+, regardless of Ballistic Skill, and never cause Morale or Pinning tests. Firing in this manner does not prevent the unit from charging, but it must charge the unit it fired upon. If casualties leave the target outside charge range, or destroy it completely, the Sons of Horus unit may not declare a charge against another enemy unit. Instead, it moves D6" directly towards the target's current position, or its final position if it was destroyed. This movement is not a charge and may not bring the unit within 1" of another enemy model. TIP OF THE SPEAR The Sons of Horus excel at decapitation strikes and sudden assaults upon the most important point in the enemy line. Even if the mission does not normally permit Deep Strike, the following may deploy using Deep Strike: • one Sons of Horus Infantry unit selected from Elites; • one Sons of Horus Independent Character, either alone or joined by a Command Squad. These units must begin the battle in Reserve. PRIDE OF THE WARMASTER The Sons of Horus possess an arrogance born of countless victories and the favour of the Warmaster himself At the end of the final scheduled game turn, the opposing player may demand that one additional complete game turn be played. Roll a D6. On a 4+, one additional complete game turn is played. This roll may only be made once per battle. This rule does not extend a mission which has already ended because a specific objective or action was completed. SONS OF HORUS ARMOURY As the favoured Legion of the Warmaster, the Sons of Horus enjoyed access to enormous reserves o

=== SONS OF HORUS NAMED ENTRIES ===

selectionEntry r25-rite-xvi-0-the-long-march | Sons Of Horus Rite of War — The Long March | type=upgrade hidden=false costs=[] cons=[('r25-rite-xvi-0-the-long-march-max', 'max', 'selections', '1', 'parent')] cats=[]
  MOD (None, 'set', 'hidden', 'true', [('lessThan', 'selections', '1', 'roster', 'legion-xvi')])
  MOD (None, 'set', 'hidden', 'true', [('lessThan', 'selections', '1', 'roster', 'allegiance-traitor')])
  RULE r25-rite-xvi-0-the-long-march-rule | The Long March | hidden=false | After Isstvan V, the Sons of Horus formed the spearhead of the Warmaster's advance upon Terra, an all-conquering host that drove relentlessly through the Imperium and swept aside world after world in its path. EFFECTS RELENTLESS MARCH At the beginning of each Sons of Horus player turn, determine the position of the majority of the surviving models in each Sons of Horus Infantry unit. The unit gains the appropriate special rule until the beginning of the next Sons of Horus player turn: • Within the Sons of Horus deployment zone — Relentless. • Outside either player's deployment zone — Fleet. • Within the enemy deployment zone — Crusader. If a unit is equally divided between two areas, the controlling player chooses which applicable rule it gains. THE WARMASTER'S PORTION During the first Sons of Horus player turn, all units in the Detachment may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1. In addition, Legion Terminator Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices. They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections. LIMITATIONS This Rite of War may only be selected by a Traitor Sons of Horus Detachment. The Detachment may not include models with the Slow and Purposeful special rule unless they begin the battle embarked aboard a Transport or enter play using Deep Strike. The Detachment may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment.

--- MATCH ---
selectionEntry r25-rite-xvi-1-the-black-reaving | Sons Of Horus Rite of War — The Black Reaving | type=upgrade hidden=false costs=[] cons=[('r25-rite-xvi-1-the-black-reaving-max', 'max', 'selections', '1', 'parent')] cats=[]
  MOD (None, 'set', 'hidden', 'true', [('lessThan', 'selections', '1', 'roster', 'legion-xvi')])
  RULE r25-rite-xvi-1-the-black-reaving-rule | The Black Reaving | hidden=false | The Black Reaving embodies the Cthonian heritage of the XVI Legion. Rather than engaging the enemy in a single ordered battle line, Reaver companies and supporting formations encircle their prey, isolate individual formations and fall upon them from several directions like the gangs of ancient Cthonia. EFFECTS REAVER ONSLAUGHT Reaver Attack Squads may be selected as Troops choices and may fulfil compulsory Troops selections. At least one of the Detachment's compulsory Troops choices must be a Reaver Attack Squad. CTHONIAN ENCIRCLEMENT After both armies have been selected, nominate up to two Sons of Horus Infantry units. These units gain the Outflank special rule. Any Dedicated Transport purchased for one of these units also gains Outflank and enters play together with its passengers. An Independent Character which begins the battle attached to one of these nominated units may Outflank with it. When one of the two nominated units enters play, the Sons of Horus player may choose the side table edge from which that unit enters instead of rolling to determine the edge. The other nominated unit follows the normal ProHammer Outflank rules. LODGE CEREMONY Once per battle, at the beginning of a Sons of Horus player turn, the Warlord may declare a Lodge Ceremony. Until the end of that player turn, all non-Vehicle Sons of Horus units gain the Fleet special rule. CUT THEM APART During the first round of a close combat, a Sons of Horus unit which charged an enemy unit that was already engaged with another friendly Sons of Horus unit may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1. This benefit applies only during the player turn in which the unit charged. LIMITATIONS The Detachment must include a Legion Centurion upgraded to a Master of Signals Consul. At least one compulsory Troops choice must be a Reaver Attack Squad. The Detachment must include at least as many Fast Attack choices as Heavy Support choices. The Detachment may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment.

Maloghurst therefore counts as the squad's Standard Bearer.   RULE r42-role-xvi-1-effects-reaver-onslaught-reaver-attack-squads-rite-note | Rite of War Interaction | hidden=false | The Black Reaving embodies the Cthonian heritage of the XVI Legion. Rather than engaging the enemy in a single ordered battle line, Reaver companies and supporting formations encircle their prey, isolate individual formations and fall upon them from several directions like the gangs of ancient Cthonia. EFFECTS REAVER ONSLAUGHT Reaver Attack Squads may be selected as Troops choices and may fulfil compulsory Troops selections. At least one of the Detachment's compulsory Troops choices must be a Reaver Attack Squad. CTHONIAN ENCIRCLEMENT After both armies have been selected, nominate up to two Sons of Horus Infantry units. These units gain the Outflank special rule. Any Dedicated Transport purchased for one of these units also gains Outflank and enters play together with its passengers. An Independent Character which begins the battle attached to one of these nominated units may Outflank with it. When one of the two nominated units enters play, the Sons of Horus player may choose the side table edge from which that unit enters instead of rolling to determine the edge. The other nominated unit follows the normal ProHammer Outflank rules. LODGE CEREMONY Once per battle, at the beginning of a Sons of Horus player turn, the Warlord may declare a Lodge Ceremony. Until the end of that player turn, all non-Vehicle Sons of Horus units gain the Fleet special rule. CUT THEM APART During the first round of a close combat, a Sons of Horus unit which charged an enemy unit that was already engaged with another friendly Sons of Horus unit may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1. This benefit applies only during the player turn in which the unit charged. LIMITATIONS The Detachment must include a Legion Centurion upgraded to a Master of Signals Consul. At least one compulsory Troops choice must be a Reaver Attack Squad. The Detachment must include at least as many Fast Attack choices as Heavy Support choices. The Detachment may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment.
